## Next cycle: profitable reconstruction before natural-world sampling

The current user priority is a complete energy-producing and repair mechanism.
Require independently usable output exceeding all formation, maintenance,
replacement, release and endowment costs, both whole-life and post-damage.
First reject any law whose conservative output ceiling cannot exceed its full
cost floor; unknown prices do not count as zero. Account finite environmental
exergy without demanding output exceed the source energy itself.

components and full-shuffle controls. Equal useful performance does not establish
an advantage from organization. Freeze opportunity, costs, damage, reconstruction
provenance, withdrawal/reserves and falsifiers before sampling. Only admit a
natural-world panel after feasibility and control opportunity are established.

## 2026-10-08 coordinated priorities — science plus bounded runtime engineering

**Documentation checkpoint, not an activation or experiment run.** This is an addendum to the **single** current plan, not a second roadmap. It incorporates [AI-Research Pass 15](https://github.com/JeremyHennessy/AI-Research/blob/8f736fd2333a80e32f722faa09f9fb218acf62aa/docs/artificial-life/78-pass15-research-handoff.md), the [research-to-Ora evidence crosswalk](https://github.com/JeremyHennessy/AI-Research/blob/main/docs/artificial-life/ora2-evidence-to-roadmap-2026-10-08.md), and INTERFACE-01 merged on Ora2.0 main at `0c712e07722a13dc8c06f7d5a935b3bec01a05e6` ([PR #19](https://github.com/JeremyHennessy/Ora2.0/pull/19); [passing CI](https://github.com/JeremyHennessy/Ora2.0/actions/runs/37827762930)). The parallel research Pass 16 branch on AI-Research is still **in progress** at this planning snapshot; do not silently treat its provisional reviews or new hypothesis as a finalized experimental result. Recheck both main heads and receipts before acting.

**Observed science baseline:** INTERFACE-01 used 16 independent initializations, two paired budgets and five correlated arms (160 receipts, **not** 160 independent evolving worlds). Under funded active conditions it recorded 109 constructed carriers and 136 installed nutrient conversions, with zero conversion in funded paid-ghost arms; all arms depleted their work bank by the frozen 64-tick endpoint. Fixed rules and retrospective external-source yokes reproduced the installed effect. The local Windows 195-test/archive acceptance was **reported**, not independently inspected here. No autonomously produced constructor, productive closure, living organism, continuously running world, learned controller, lineage inheritance or self-sustaining maintenance has been demonstrated.

### Track A — leading scientific hypothesis (current next study remains unchanged)

1. **Constructor/production-network design gate.** Before another simulation, preregister a finite material/work-conserving law in which *the system pays to produce a constructor that is causally required for interface assembly*, and the producer's own dependencies are independently logged. Register an unscreened generator, actual natural opportunity denominators, targeted constructor knockout, paid matched impairment, external constructor/source, fixed/ghost and low-exposure controls. Do not retune INTERFACE-01 or revive the prior sparse CLOSURE-03 repair assay.
2. **Within-life history versus across-generation evolution (later independent gates).** Pass 15 EERC-L v5 proposes separating changes to a continuing entity after distinct randomized histories at identical current genotype/environment from inherited changes in *which latent transitions are accessible*. Test both against passive relaxation, fixed filters/integrators, preinstalled switching, genotype sorting, full accounted cost and unexpected resource reversals. This is a question, not an imported memory/learning module.
3. **Functional descendants and open-ended ecology (conditional later gates).** Require independently viable daughter/granddaughter organizations that reproduce *a causally useful collective function*, with no privileged automatic group copy; only then assess transfer, ecological novelty and plateau/extinction. Do not score consciousness or a single artificial-life grade from uptime, population or attractive renderings.

### Track B — parallel persistent-world engineering **planning**, not runtime authorization

Engineering continuity should not wait for a scientific verdict, but the runtime must not be used to make an unproven substrate appear alive.

1. **Heartbeat contract / bounded dry run:** design one authoritative writer and immutable world/run/config ID; separate monotonic `simulation_tick` from wall-clock `last_heartbeat`; emit state/trace hashes and truthful `running/paused/stopped/failed` status. A timer firing without an advanced, audited state is not a simulation heartbeat. Prototype only in an isolated, explicitly operator-launched finite fixture on a separate branch, without changing existing experimental semantics.
2. **Schema-specific persistence:** reuse the architectural lessons of RUNTIME-01's reported Windows checkpoint/restart acceptance, **not** its AL01 state schema as universal proof. Before calling a selected new world persistent, inject before/after-journal/checkpoint crashes; verify exact replay, corruption rejection, no double transition, single-writer lock, pause/stop, disk/CPU caps and source-identity checks. Keep original scientific runs and seeds untouched.
3. **Observer contract:** build a read-only viewer from validated telemetry; distinguish simulation tick rate, latest *verified* snapshot, age/staleness, process health, material/work flows, constructions/failures and, only where actually supported, lineage. The UI must never mint actions/state, conceal stopped worlds, or change approved visual/layout behavior incidentally.
4. **Unattended activation remains gated:** verify off-`D:` independent backups, actual power-loss recovery, unprivileged isolation, operator stop controls, quota exhaustion, recovery on a disposable copy and explicit separate approval before persistent autonomous scheduling. No paid/model API, public self-hosted runner, automatic execution of GitHub commits or external actions is authorized by this documentation.

**Progress accounting:** Track A outputs a falsifiable scientific result, including negatives; Track B outputs independently verified runtime reliability. A successful process heartbeat is **not** learning, and an authored carrier or fixed-feedback filter is **not** an independently evolved organism. Keep AI-Research source claims and evolving Pass 16 notes separate from Ora code and measured results. Preserve source/protocol/historical hashes, the earlier Ora/AgentTest separation, approved UI baselines and CLOSURE-03 reserved seeds `8000..8063`. There is no delivery-date or scientific-success guarantee.

## User-facing run summaries (standing preference)

After every run, provide a plain-English, self-contained summary: the question
tested; exactly what ran (development, held-out or authored fixture); observed
outcomes and denominators; what the evidence supports and cannot establish;
any failed checks or inactive controls; and the next concrete step with its
reason. Link the detailed receipt instead of overwhelming the user with hashes.
Distinguish software validation from scientific results and coupon counts from
independent populations. Report unsuccessful attempts too. This is a reporting
preference, not authorization for unattended scheduling or recurring execution.

## Decision from CLOSURE-02

Retire ongoing M synthesis as the leading endogenous-boundary candidate under this regime. Active M improves recovery relative to inert M, but shell vs ghost does **not establish an advantage** (it is not an equivalence test); disabling synthesis improves recovery markedly. Event costs match, trajectory-total costs and altered selection probabilities do not. No life, heredity, individuality, energy metabolism or intelligence was demonstrated. Preserve the model as a negative/control substrate; no tuning of its parameters on these seeds.

See [complete evidence, retained integration failure and confounds](CLOSURE-02-RESULTS.md). Keep original study source and archive bytes unchanged.

Direct primary-method check: [Mathis et al., AlChemy v2, 23 August 2024](https://arxiv.org/html/2408.12137v2), especially sections 4.2–5.2 and 6. The study reports that stable aggregate diversity can hide changes in expression membership, higher-order combinations often fail, and initialization/standardization changes outcomes. Its formal CRN construction is not a demonstration that random worlds find a viable metabolism. This is published/manuscript evidence **reported by those authors**, not reproduced by Ora2.0.

Design implications:
- Count restoration of specific causal functions and their producers, not diversity/persistence alone.
- Vary the object generator as a prespecified factor, without selecting successful worlds.
- Separate resource access from construction of a new usable niche.
- The new [Pass-10 transition synthesis](https://github.com/JeremyHennessy/AI-Research/blob/599e4f1b763a6dc3f3573e87c82ca06f3e8c69f1/docs/artificial-life/47-pass10-major-transition-mechanism-synthesis.md) adds an explicit later gate: independently viable group daughters must transmit collective function; temporary clustering, researcher-separated propagules and parameter-weakened scaffolding do not establish endogenous reproductive individuality. These are reviewed author-reported studies and a prospective hypothesis, not Ora reproduction. Keep this gate separate from CLOSURE-03 repair.
- Keep living organization, individual learning and consciousness as distinct hypotheses. Changing a catalog classification is not an evolved computational capability.

## Local Codex handoff

No immediate desktop experiment or installation is needed. Following manual review of a merged source revision, local Codex may use the existing laboratory helper to:
- fetch/review changes without auto-executing them;
- pin source and run bounded regression acceptance in a new directory using existing lock, minimum-space and 300-second limits;
- preserve the original/replay CLOSURE-02 ZIPs and source snapshots on the laboratory drive, verify their hashes and same-drive restoration, and log exact paths privately;
- obtain and test an independent off-drive backup destination when available.

All persistent local laboratory files remain on D:. Do not execute fetched code on the desktop without its reviewed local acceptance step. No reinstall, paid service, metered API, OpenAI API/LLM requirement, self-hosted runner, host self-modification, continuous worker or unattended scheduling. Runtime-01's reactor schema does not make process objects or spatial worlds resumable.
